But is the SOTA LP correct and effective? Let's derive and maybe adapt.

Given current f≥0, sum=√(2N), M=||f*f||∞. Solve LP:
max ∑ b_j s.t. b_j≥0, (f*b)_k ≤ M for all k=0..2N-1.
Then rescale b to sum√(2N): b_res = S/sum(b)*b. Update f_new = (1-t)f + t b_res.
Why does this reduce M? Let's test with simple N=3 maybe. Suppose f uniform, M=2 (for S^2=6? uniform height=√(6/3)=√2=1.414, conv max=3? Wait sum S=√6~2.449, uniform a=S/N=0.816, M=N*a^2=6/3=2. yes). LP f*b≤2. For uniform f, constraints f*b conv = (S/N)* (b shifted sums) ≤2. That means sliding window sums of b≤2N/S? For uniform f=N? Let f=F constant. f*b coefficient = F * sum over pairs b_i b? Let's examine SOTA method mathematically and possible improvements.

Given f, solve max ∑ b subject f*b ≤M. Since f*b min and max linear. Let b feasible. If we normalize b to S, then f = (1-t)f + t b. We want (new)*(new) ≤ M? Expand:
new^2 = (1-t)^2 f^2 + t(1-t)(f*b + b*f) + t^2 b^2.
We know f^2≤M, f*b≤M by LP. b*f? Since convolution commutative, b*f = f*b (commutative!) So cross ≤M. But b^2 not controlled. For t small, if t less than something: M_new ≤ (1-t)^2 M + 2t(1-t)M + t^2 max(b^2). The first three sum = (1-t^2)M. Need t^2 max(b^2)≤t^2 M to keep ≤M, i.e., require b^2≤M. LP doesn't guarantee. But maybe rescaling b to sum S ensures ∥b^2∥∞≤? Not from f*b≤M. However perhaps because b is extreme point with sum max, and nonnegative, can show b^2≤M? Maybe not.

Update with t small: M_new ≈ M - t^2(∥f^2∥? Hmm) plus t^2 ∥b^2∥. If ∥b^2∥ ≤ M + O(t), may not decrease. Let's compute directional derivative of M: at t=0, M'(0) = max( gradient of c_f in direction d where c_f=M ) = max_k ∑ active ∂c_k/∂f_i d_i. Since d=b-S? Actually rescaled sum fixed d=sum0. Feasibility f*b≤M may imply derivative ≤0 for active k? For each active k in f^2, derivative = (f*b)_k ≤M? But active c_k=M, and f*b_k? Since (f^2)_k≤M and f*b_k≤M, derivative can be positive if f*b_k=M. So not guarantee descent. Maybe second order.

Subgradient method:

Full multiplicative algorithm pseudocode:
```
def refine_multiplicative(p, time_limit, seed):
    N=len(p); S=np.sum(p)
    p = p.copy()/S*S_target
    best = p.copy(); best_score=fast_score(p)
    beta = 5.0
    eta = 0.1
    for it in range(num_iter):
        q = self_conv(p)
        M = max(q)
        # if using target floor: q_shift = q - (M*0.98?)
        weights = exp(beta*(q - M))
        weights /= sum(weights)
        A = 2*adjoint(weights, p)
        maxA = max(A)
        if maxA > 0:
            z = -eta * (A/maxA)
            # optionally subtract weighted mean to keep sum? renormalize anyway
            p *= np.exp(z)
        p = np.maximum(p, floor)
        p *= S_target/p.sum()
        score = fast_score(p)
        if score < best_score: best=p
        # adap eta/beta
    return best
```

Let's design exact functions:
```
def _fft_power_of_two(n):
    p=1
    while p<n: p*=2
    return p

def self_convolution(a):
    n=len(a); L=_fft_power_of_two(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    q=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:2*n-1]
    # eliminate tiny negative
    np.maximum(q, 0.0, out=q)
    return q
```
Note: For sequence values ~0.02, q ~1, FFT round errors ~1e-12. max okay.

`adjoint_self_weights(w, p)`:
```
def adjoint_self_weights(w, p):
    n=len(p)
    L=_fft_power_of_two(3*n-2) # full length
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fw*fp, L)[:3*n-2]
    return c[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Need verify length: w length 2n-1, p length n. Full length 3n-2. A_i length n at c[n-1:n-1+n]. Check with small N=2 above. Good.

```
def block_objective_gradient(z, N, beta, lower? ) -> (F, grad)
    z = np.asarray(z)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    m = np.exp(z); m /= m.sum()
    K=m.size
    # p density
    p = np.empty(N)
    starts = np.arange(K+1)*N//K
    for b in range(K):
        p[starts[b]:starts[b+1]] = m[b] / max(1, starts[b+1]-starts[b])
    q=self_conv(p)
    qmax=q.max()
    if not finite: ...
    w=np.exp(beta*(q-qmax)); w/=w.sum()
    A=2*adjoint(w,p)
    B = np.zeros(K)
    for b: B[b]=np.sum(A[starts[b]:starts[b+1]])
    meanB = np.dot(m,B)
    grad = m*(B - meanB)
    F = qmax + np.log(w.sum())? Wait w already normalized? F = qmax + log(Z)/beta. If w normalized, Z = sum exp(beta(q-qmax)) before normalization; track Z.
    return F, grad
```

```
def block_initials(K, rng, N, seed):

    candidates=[]
```

Let's design full multiplicative refinement with multi-start and annealing in a function:
```
def refine_full(p, seed, time_limit):
    targetS = sqrt(2*N)
    p = normalize_to_sum(p, targetS)
    best = p.copy(); best_score = fast_score(p)
    beta=10.0; eta=0.05
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time up break
       q=self_conv(p)
       M=max(q)
       # Choose weights; if M close target, use a slightly lower shift to keep active broad? qmax?
       e=np.exp(beta*(q-M)); Z=e.sum(); w=e/Z
       A=2*adjoint(w,p)
       maxA=A.max()
       if maxA<=0: break
       # To avoid monotone drift, center A by weighted mean weighted by p? Multiplicative p*=exp(-eta A/maxA) then norm; centered by subtracting mean(w over i)? Use A - p weighted mean maybe.
       meanA = np.dot(p,A)/np.sum(p)
       A = A - meanA  # under exp then renorm approx softmax update
       # scale by max positive?
       scale = np.max(np.abs(A))
       z=-eta*(A/scale)
       p *= np.exp(z)
       p = np.maximum(p, floor)
       p *= targetS/p.sum()
       score=fast_score(p)
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best=p.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0
       else: no_improve +=1
       # adapt eta/beta
       if it % 50 ==0: eta = ...
       if no_improve > 200:
           # perturb from best or reset
           if no_improve>500: p=best.copy(); no_improve=0; beta=20; eta=0.02
           else: p = apply_block_perturbation(best,rng)
    return best
```

Let's write helper `random_fourier_density(K, N, rng)`:
```
def make_fourier_density(N, M, rng, scale):
    x = np.arange(N)/N
    z = np.zeros(N)
    # include linear slope random
    z += rng.normal(0, scale*0.5)*x
    for m in range(1, M+1):
       amp = scale / m
       z += amp*rng.normal(0,1)*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x) + amp*rng.normal(0,1)*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x)
    # ensure positive
    # shift z to make max 0? p=exp(z - min(z)) maybe; but if range large, spikes.
    z = z - z.max()
    p = np.exp(z)
    p = p/(p.sum())
    return p
```
This can create very uneven if scale>2. Use scale 0.5-2. For N=4498, cos generation many maybe. We'll generate a few.

Custom gradient descent for block `z`:
```
def optimize_block_z(z, N, starts, beta, maxiter, time_deadline, rng):
    z = z.copy(); z -= z.mean()
    best_z=z.copy(); best_F=block_Fgrad(z,...)[0]
    step = 0.2
    momentum = np.zeros_like(z)
    for it in range(maxiter):
       if time>deadline: break
       F,grad = block_Fgrad(z,N,starts,beta)
       if not finite: break
       if F < best_F: best_F=F; best_z=z.copy()
       # line search with several alphas
       chosen=None
       for a in [step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.125]:
          ztrial = z - a*grad
          ztrial -= ztrial.mean()
          Ftrial,_ = block_Fgrad(ztrial,N,starts,beta)
          if Ftrial < F:
             chosen=trial,Ftrial; break
       if chosen is None:
          step*=0.5
          continue
       # update momentum? z = chosen
       z=chosen; F=Ftrial
       step = min(1.0, step*1.05)
    return best_z, best_F
```

Let's design `refine_full` detailed:
```
def refine_full(p0, rng, deadline, N, S, target_score=1.505):
    p = normalize_sum(p0, S)
    floor = 1e-12 * max(1.0, S/N)
    best = p.copy(); best_score = float(self_convolution(p).max())  # M
    beta = 20.0
    eta = 0.1
    stall = 0
    it=0
    last_print=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it +=1
        q = self_convolution(p)
        M = float(q.max())
        if M < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = M; best = p.copy(); stall=0
        else:
            stall +=1
        if it % 100 ==0 and time.monotonic()-last_print > 5: print; last_print=...
        if best_score <= 1.50275: maybe continue to improve but can break if below target? Lower better; don't break unless very low? We can break if best_score < 1.5001? But hard lower? target1.5027, just continue a bit. For time, if below target, we can reduce iterations? Maybe return.
        # weights
        shift = max(target_score, M - 1.0/beta)
        if shift < M - 1e-6: shift = M - 1e-6
        e = np.exp(beta*(q - shift))
        Z = e.sum()
        if Z <= 0 or not finite:
            beta = 10; continue
        w = e / Z
        A = 2.0 * adjoint_self_weights(w, p)
        A = A - np.dot(p, A)/S  # center weighted mean
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(A)))
        if scale <= 1e-14: break
        # If all A negative after center? max abs.
        z = -eta * (A / scale)
        # prevent huge exponent: clip z to [-3,3]? If eta=0.1, z within 0.1 actually A/scale in [-1,1], so safe. If eta maybe 1.0.
        p *= np.exp(z)
        p = np.maximum(p, floor)
        p *= S / p.sum()
        # occasional random perturb
        if stall > 100:
            # try a random block from best
            if stall > 300:
                p = best.copy()
                p = random_block_update(p, rng, 0.3, S, N)
                stall=0; beta=min(200.0, beta*1.5); eta=0.05
            else:
                p = random_block_update(p, rng, 0.1, S, N)
        # adapt beta/eta
        if stall > 50:
            eta = max(0.01, eta*0.8)
            if M - best_score < 1e-3:
                beta = min(500.0, beta*1.2)
        if stall == 0:
            eta = min(0.3, eta*1.05)
            if best_score < 1.6:
                beta = min(500.0, beta*1.02)
    return best, best_score
```

`random_block_update`: Add small noise to log p:
```
def random_block_update(p, rng, sigma, S, N):
   qn = rng.integers(1, 4)
   for _ in range(qn):
      width = int(rng.integers(max(1,N//500), max(2,N//20)))
      st = int(rng.integers(0,N-width))
      p[st:st+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma, width))
   p = np.clip(p, floor, None)
   p *= S/p.sum()
   return p
```
Need floor parameter. Maybe pass floor.

Local random hill-climb on global best:
```
def local_hillclimb(p, rng, deadline, N,S):
   best=p.copy(); best_score=best max
   stall=0
   while time < deadline:
      cand=random_block_update(best,rng, 0.1+0.4*min(1,stall/200), S,N)
      score=max self_conv(cand)
      if score<best_score:
          best=cand; best_score=score; stall=0
      else:
          stall+=1
    return best,best_score
```

`make_fourier_density`:
```
def make_fourier_density(N, M, rng, scale):
    x = np.arange(N, dtype=float) / float(N)
    z = np.zeros(N)
    # slope
    z += scale * 0.7 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) * (x - 0.5)
    # random boundary exponent maybe
    for m in range(1, M+1):
        amp = scale / float(m)
        c = amp * rng.normal(0.0,1.0)
        s = amp * rng.normal(0.0,1.0)
        if c != 0: z += c*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x)
        if s != 0: z += s*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x)
    z -= np.max(z)
    # shift by min? to avoid all tiny? exp OK if min -range; maybe range up to 3scale.
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite: p=np.ones(N)
    else: p /= s
    return p
```
Scale up to maybe3; range ~9, exp big but max1, min e^-9 fine. Good.

Block initial starts:
```
def block_initial_starts(K, rng, base_density=None, N=None):
   ts=(np.arange(K)+0.5)/float(K)
   starts_list=[]
   if base_density is not None: # base_density sum1
       # compute block means
       means = []
       for i in range(K): segment = base_density[floor(i*N/K):floor((i+1)*N/K)]
       means.append(max(segment.mean(), 1e-12))
       z = np.log(means); z-=np.mean(z); starts_list.append(z + rng.normal(0,0.01,K))
   # uniform with small noise
   starts_list.append(rng.normal(0,0.02,K))
   # slopes
   for a in [-2,-1,-0.5,0.5,1,2]:
       z=a*(ts-0.5)+rng.normal(0,0.02,K); starts_list.append(z)
   # polynomials random coeffs
   for i in range(8):
       a=rng.normal(0,1); b=rng.normal(0,1); c=rng.normal(0,0.5)
       z=a*(ts-0.5)+b*(ts-0.5)**2+c*(ts-0.5)**3; starts_list.append(z)
   # Fourier random
   for i in range(10):
       z=np.zeros(K)
       for m in [1,2,3,4]:
          amp=rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)/m
          z+=amp*rng.normal(0,1)*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*ts)
          z+=amp*rng.normal(0,1)*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*ts)
       z+=rng.normal(0,0.3)*(ts-0.5)
       starts_list.append(z)
   # two-level patterns
   r1=int(rng.integers(1,K)); r2=int(rng.integers(1,K))
   for pattern in ...:
   return starts_list
```
Keep maybe 24 starts. Sort unique? Not needed.

```
def run_block_phase(N,S,rng,deadline,base_density=None):
    K = 64 if N>=320 else 16; maybe min(64, N//8)
    K = int(min(64, max(16, N//32)))? For 4498 N//32=140, max16 min64=>64.
    starts = block_starts(N,K)
    base_dens = None
    if base_density is not None:
       base_dens = base_density / base_density.sum()
    inits = block_initial_starts(K, rng, base_dens, N)
    beta=20.0
    results=[]
    per_start_time? We'll simply loop while time and index < len(inits). Maybe stop if deadline.
    maxiter = 200 if HAVE_SCIPY else 100

       z = optimize_block_z(z0, N, starts, beta, maxiter, deadline, rng)
       dens = block_to_density(z,N,starts)
       dens = np.maximum(dens,0); sum ...

       if finite: results.append((score, dens*S))
       # also maybe smoothed version of dens: lowpass FFT
    results.sort key score; keep top 10
    return [c for score,c in results[:10]]
```

```
def optimize_block_z(z0,N,starts,beta,maxiter,deadline,rng):
   z = np.asarray(z0,float).copy(); z -= z.mean()
   if HAVE_SCIPY:
       problem=BlockProblem(N,starts,beta)
       try:
          res = opt.minimize(problem, z, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8})
          if np.isfinite(res.fun) and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)): return res.x
       except: pass
   # fallback custom gradient
   return custom_gradient_block(...)
```

Custom fallback:
```
def custom_gradient_block(z,N,starts,beta,maxiter,deadline,rng):
   z = z.copy(); z-=z.mean()
   best_z=z.copy(); Fbest, grad = block_fgrad(...); Fbest=F
   lr=0.1
   for it in range(maxiter):
       if time>deadline: break
       F,grad=...
       if finite and F<Fbest: ...
       # line search
       improved=False
       for factor in (1,0.5,0.25,0.125):
          trial=z - lr*factor*grad; trial-=trial.mean()
          Ft,_=block_fgrad(trial,...)
          if Ft < F - 1e-12:
             z=trial; F=Ft; lr=min(1.0, lr*1.2); improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          lr*=0.5
   return best_z
```
Use `maxiter=100`.

`block_to_density` with starts. `starts` is np.array length K+1.
```
def block_to_density(z,N,starts):
   z = z - z.mean(); z -= z.max(); m=np.exp(z); m=m/m.sum()
   p=np.zeros(N)
   for k in range(len(m)):
      l=starts[k+1]-starts[k]
      if l>0: p[starts[k]:starts[k+1]]=m[k]/l
   psum=p.sum()
   if psum<=0: p=np.ones(N)/N
   else: p/=psum
   return p
```

`block_fgrad`: Need handle starts as np array. `np.add.reduceat(A, starts[:-1])` works if starts length K+1. For K=1: starts[:-1]=[0]; reduceat returns [sum A from0 toend]. Good. But if starts has last not N? It is N.
```
B = np.zeros(K)
for b in range(K):
    B[b]=np.sum(A[starts[b]:starts[b+1]])
```

```
def random_block_update(p, rng, sigma, S, N, floor):
   q = rng.integers(1,4)
   for _ in range(q):
```

```
def smooth_linear(p, sigma):
    w = int(2*np.ceil(3*sigma))+1
    if w%2==0: w+=1
    if w <3: w=3
    x=np.arange(w)-w//2
    kernel=np.exp(-x*x/(2*sigma*sigma)); kernel/=kernel.sum()
    ps=np.convolve(p,kernel,mode='same')  # direct length N, kernel small; 4498*100 ~0.45M okay if few
    ps=np.maximum(ps,0); s=ps.sum(); if s>0: ps*=S/s
    return ps
```

In refine, after local bill, maybe add candidate to hall. Use hall in final.

`local_hillclimb` function:
```
def local_hillclimb(p, rng, deadline, N, S, floor):
    best=np.asarray(p, dtype=float).copy(); best_score=float(self_convolution(best).max())
    stall=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        sigma = 0.05 + 0.3*(min(1.0, stall/300.0))
        cand = random_block_update(best, rng, sigma, S, N, floor)
        # occasionally smoothing
          score = self_convolution(cand).max()
        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best=cand; best_score=score; stall=0
        else:
           stall += 1
        if stall > 50:
           p_try = smooth_linear(best, 2.0, S, N)
           score_try = self_convolution(p_try).max()
           if score_try < best_score:
              best=p_try; best_score=score_try; stall=0
    return best, best_score
```
Smoothing inside each stall may be expensive but occasional.

```
if w%2==0: w+=1
if w<3: return p.copy()

x=np.arange(w)-w//2
kernel=np.exp(-(x*x)/(2.0*sigma*sigma)); kernel/=kernel.sum()
ps=np.convolve(p,kernel,mode='same')
ps=np.maximum(ps,0); s=ps.sum()
if s>0: ps*=S/s
return ps
```

```python
"""
Search improvements for the nonnegative self-convolution peak problem.

The program works on a fixed length N (usually the length of height_sequence_1,
falling back to 4498).  It constructs several diversified starting densities,
optimizes piecewise-constant block densities with a smooth log-sum-exp
approximation of the peak-convolution objective, then refines the full
sequence with an active-set multiplicative log-sum-exp iteration.  The best
sequence is finally verified by the authoritative evaluate_sequence() routine.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _scipy_opt
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False


def _fft_len(n):
    p = 1
    n = int(n)
    while p < n:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def self_convolution(a):
    """Linear convolution a * a by FFT, returning the 2n-1 coefficients."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = _fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def adjoint_self_weights(w, p):
    """
    Given weights w_k on the convolution lags k=0..2n-2 and a sequence p,
    compute A_i = sum_k w_k p_{k-i}, i=0..n-1.
    """
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    L = _fft_len(3 * n - 2)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p[::-1], L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fw * fp, L)[:3 * n - 2]
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(w[0] * p[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    return c[n - 1:n - 1 + n].copy()


def normalize_sum(a, target):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(a.size, target / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a *= float(target) / s
    return a


def fast_score(a):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    q = self_convolution(a)
    m = float(q.max())
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def simple_score_fixed_sum(a, S):
    """Score assuming sum(a)==S; since score = M in that normalization."""
    q = self_convolution(a)
    m = float(q.max())
    return m if np.isfinite(m) else float("inf")


def make_fallback(N):
    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * N))
    return np.full(int(N), S / float(N), dtype=np.float64)


def make_uniform_density(N):
    return np.full(int(N), 1.0 / float(N), dtype=np.float64)


def make_geometric_density(N, a):
    x = np.arange(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
    p = np.exp(a * x)
    s = p.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return make_uniform_density(N)
    return p / s


def make_fourier_density(N, M, rng, scale):
    x = np.arange(N, dtype=np.float64) / float(N)
    z = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    z += scale * 0.7 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) * (x - 0.5)
    for m in range(1, M + 1):
        amp = scale / float(m)
        c = amp * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
        s = amp * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
        if c != 0.0:
            z += c * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x)
        if s != 0.0:
            z += s * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x)
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return make_uniform_density(N)
    return p / s


def block_starts(N, K):
    K = int(max(1, K))
    N = int(N)
    return (np.arange(K + 1, dtype=np.int64) * N) // K


def block_to_density(z, N, starts):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    m = np.exp(z)
    sm = float(m.sum())
    if sm <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sm):
        return make_uniform_density(N)
    m /= sm
    p = np.zeros(int(N), dtype=np.float64)
    for b in range(len(m)):
        l = int(starts[b + 1] - starts[b])
        if l > 0:
            p[starts[b]:starts[b + 1]] = m[b] / float(l)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return make_uniform_density(N)
    return p / s


def block_fgrad(z, N, starts, beta):
    """Smooth log-sum-exp objective and gradient w.r.t. block log-masses."""
    K = len(starts) - 1
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    z -= float(np.max(z))
    m = np.exp(z)
    sm = float(m.sum())
    if sm <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sm):
        return (1e300, np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64))
    m /= sm

    p = np.zeros(int(N), dtype=np.float64)
    for b in range(K):
        l = int(starts[b + 1] - starts[b])
        if l > 0:
            p[starts[b]:starts[b + 1]] = m[b] / float(l)

    ps = float(p.sum())
    if ps <= 0.0:
        return (1e300, np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64))
    p /= ps

    q = self_convolution(p)
    qmax = float(q.max())
    if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 0.0:
        return (1e300, np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64))

    e = np.exp(beta * (q - qmax))
    Z = float(e.sum())
    if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
        return (1e300, np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64))
    w = e / Z

    A = 2.0 * adjoint_self_weights(w, p)
    B = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    for b in range(K):
        B[b] = float(np.sum(A[starts[b]:starts[b + 1]]))
    mean_B = float(np.dot(m, B))
    grad = m * (B - mean_B)
    return (float(qmax + np.log(Z) / beta), grad)


class BlockObjective:
    def __init__(self, N, starts, beta):
        self.N = int(N)
        self.starts = starts
        self.beta = float(beta)

    def __call__(self, z):
        return block_fgrad(z, self.N, self.starts, self.beta)


def block_gradient_descent(z, N, starts, beta, maxiter, deadline):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    best = z.copy()
    F, _ = block_fgrad(z, N, starts, beta)
    best_F = F
    eps = 1e-16 / max(1.0, float(np.linalg.norm(z)) + 1.0)
    direction = None

    for _ in range(maxiter):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        F, grad = block_fgrad(z, N, starts, beta)
        if not np.isfinite(F) or not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
            break
        if F < best_F:
            best_F = F
            best = z.copy()

        nz = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if nz <= 1e-14:
            direction = rng_direction = make_uniform_density(N)
            step = 0.05
        else:
            direction = -grad / nz
            step = 1.0
            F_best_trial = np.inf
            improved = False
            for _ls in range(8):
                trial = z + step * direction
                trial -= float(np.mean(trial))
                Ft, _ = block_fgrad(trial, N, starts, beta)
                if np.isfinite(Ft) and Ft < F_best_trial:
                    F_best_trial = Ft
                if np.isfinite(Ft) and Ft <= F - 1e-13:
                    improved = True
                    break
                step *= 0.5
            if not improved or not np.isfinite(F_best_trial):
                break
            z = trial
            F = F_best_trial
            if F < best_F:
                best_F = F
                best = z.copy()

    return best


def optimize_block_z(z0, N, starts, beta, maxiter, deadline, rng):
    z = np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    if _HAVE_SCIPY:
        try:
            obj = BlockObjective(N, starts, beta)
            res = _scipy_opt.minimize(
                obj,
                z,
                jac=True,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                    "ftol": 1e-12,
                    "gtol": 1e-8,
                    "maxls": 20,
                },
            )
            if hasattr(res, "x") and np.all(np.isfinite(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64))):
                out = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                out -= float(np.mean(out))
                if np.isfinite(res.fun):
                    return out
        except Exception:
            pass
    return block_gradient_descent(z, N, starts, beta, int(maxiter), deadline)


def block_initial_starts(K, rng, base_density, N):
    ts = (np.arange(K, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(K)
    out = []

    if base_density is not None:
        d = np.asarray(base_density, dtype=np.float64)
        d = np.maximum(d, 0.0)
        s = d.sum()
        if s > 0.0:
            d /= s
            starts = block_starts(N, K)
            means = []
            for b in range(K):
                seg = d[starts[b]:starts[b + 1]]
                means.append(max(float(seg.mean()), 1e-14))
            z = np.log(np.asarray(means, dtype=np.float64))
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            out.append(z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, size=K))

    out.append(rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, size=K))
    out.append(np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64))

    for a in (-3.0, -2.0, -1.0, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
        z = a * (ts - 0.5) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, size=K)
        out.append(z)

    for _ in range(10):
        a = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
        b = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
        c = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5)
        z = a * (ts - 0.5) + b * (ts - 0.5) ** 2 + c * (ts - 0.5) ** 3
        out.append(z)

    for _ in range(12):
        z = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        for mm in (1, 2, 3, 4):
            amp = rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0) / float(mm)
            z += amp * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * mm * ts)
            z += amp * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * mm * ts)
        z += rng.normal(0.0, 0.4) * (ts - 0.5)
        out.append(z)

    r1 = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, K)))
    r2 = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, K)))
    for pattern in range(4):
        z = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        if pattern == 0:
            z[:r1] = 1.5
            z[rr := r1:rr + max(1, (K - r1) // 2)] = 0.5
        elif pattern == 1:
            z[:r1] = 0.5
            z[:r2] += 1.0
        elif pattern == 2:
            z[:] = np.linspace(1.0, max(0.1, 1.0 - 1.5 * rng.random()), K)
        else:
            z[:] = np.exp(np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, K))
        z -= float(np.mean(z))
        z += rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, size=K)
        out.append(z)

    return out


def run_block_phase(N, S, rng, deadline, base_density):
    K = int(min(64, max(16, N // 32)))
    starts = block_starts(N, K)
    inits = block_initial_starts(K, rng, base_density, N)

    results = []
    beta = 20.0
    max_iter = 70 if _HAVE_SCIPY else 45

    for idx, z0 in enumerate(inits):
        if time.monotonic() + 5.0 >= deadline:
            break
        z = optimize_block_z(z0, N, starts, beta, max_iter, deadline, rng)
        dens = block_to_density(z, N, starts)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(dens)):
            continue
        raw = normalize_sum(dens, S)
        sc = simple_score_fixed_sum(raw, S)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            results.append((float(sc), raw.copy()))
        if idx > 0 and idx % 4 == 0:
            if results:
                results.sort(key=_item_score)
                print(f"  block phase stops={idx} best_block={results[0][0]:.9f}", flush=True)
                if len(results) > 12:
                    del results[12:]

    if results:
        results.sort(key=_item_score)
        return [c for _, c in results[:12]]
    return []


def random_block_update(p, rng, sigma, S, N, floor):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    nblocks = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
    for _ in range(nblocks):
        minw = max(1, int(N // 1000))
        maxw = max(minw + 1, int(N // 20))
        width = int(rng.integers(minw, maxw + 1))
        width = min(width, int(N // 2))
        if width <= 0:
            continue
        hi = max(1, N - width)
        st = int(rng.integers(0, hi + 1))
        p[st:st + width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=width))
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        p *= float(S) / s
    else:
        p = make_fallback(N)
    return p


def smooth_linear(p, sigma, S, N):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    width = int(2 * np.ceil(3.0 * sigma)) + 1
    if width % 2 == 0:
        width += 1
    if width < 3:
        return normalize_sum(p, S)
    width = min(width, 2 * N - 1)
    if width % 2 == 0:
        width -= 1
    x = np.arange(width, dtype=np.float64) - (width // 2)
    kernel = np.exp(-(x * x) / (2.0 * float(sigma) * float(sigma)))
    ks = float(kernel.sum())
    if ks <= 0.0:
        return normalize_sum(p, S)
    kernel /= ks
    q = np.convolve(p, kernel, mode="same")
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return normalize_sum(p, S)
    q *= float(S) / s
    return q


def refine_full(p0, rng, deadline, N, S, target_score=1.505):
    p = normalize_sum(p0, S)
    floor = 1e-12 * max(1.0, S / float(N))
    q0 = self_convolution(p)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = float(q0.max())
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best = make_fallback(N)
        best_score = fast_score(best)

    beta = 30.0
    eta = 0.18
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        q = self_convolution(p)
        M = float(q.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            break
        if M < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = M
            best = p.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        if it % 400 == 0 and (time.monotonic() - last_print > 4.0):
            print(f"    full refine it={it} M={M:.9f} best={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)
            last_print = time.monotonic()

        if best_score <= 1.50001:
            # Very close to the apparent asymptotic barrier; keep a short polish.
            if it > 800:
                break

        if best_score > 1.503:
            target_M = 1.505
        else:
            target_M = max(1.4990, best_score - 0.0020)

        shift = max(float(target_M), M - 1.0 / beta)
        if shift >= M:
            shift = M - min(1e-6, 0.01 / beta)
        e = np.exp(beta * (q - shift))
        Z = float(e.sum())
        if Z <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(Z):
            beta = max(15.0, 0.6 * beta)
            continue

        w = e / Z
        A = 2.0 * adjoint_self_weights(w, p)
        psum = float(p.sum())
        if psum <= 0.0:
            break
        A = A - float(np.dot(p, A)) / psum
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(A)))
        if scale <= 1e-14:
            p = random_block_update(best, rng, 0.25, S, N, floor)
            stall = 0
            beta = max(15.0, 0.65 * beta)
            continue

        log_step = -eta * (A / scale)
        np.clip(log_step, -3.0, 3.0, out=log_step)
        p *= np.exp(log_step)
        p = np.maximum(p, floor)
        psum = float(p.sum())
        if psum <= 0.0:
            p = normalize_sum(best, S)
        else:
            p *= float(S) / psum

        if stall > 250:
            p = random_block_update(best, rng, 0.30, S, N, floor)
            beta = max(15.0, 0.7 * beta)
            eta = 0.12
            stall = 0
        elif stall > 80:
            beta = max(15.0, 0.85 * beta)
            eta = max(0.05, 0.85 * eta)
        else:
            if best_score < 1.56:
                beta = min(450.0, beta + 4.0)
            eta = min(0.55, eta * 1.02)

    return best, best_score


def hill_climb_local(p0, rng, deadline, N, S):
    p = normalize_sum(p0, S)
    floor = 1e-12 * max(1.0, S / float(N))
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = simple_score_fixed_sum(best, S)
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() + 3.0 < deadline:
        it += 1
        sigma = 0.06 + 0.35 * min(1.0, float(stall) / 350.0)
        cand = random_block_update(best, rng, sigma, S, N, floor)
        sc = simple_score_fixed_sum(cand, S)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best = cand
            best_score = sc
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        if stall % 40 == 0:
            for sig in (1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
                cand2 = smooth_linear(best, float(sig), S, N)
                sc2 = simple_score_fixed_sum(cand2, S)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best = cand2
                    best_score = sc2
                    stall = 0
                    break

    return best, best_score


def _item_score(item):
    return item[0]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Entry point: search a fixed-length nonnegative sequence and return it."""
    print("Starting fixed-length nonnegative convolution search", flush=True)
    shutdown = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0)

    N = 4498
    base = None
    try:
        h = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
        if isinstance(h, (list, tuple, np.ndarray)):
            arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
            if arr.ndim == 1 and arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
                arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if arr.sum() > 0.01:
                    base = arr
    except Exception:
        base = None

    if base is not None:
        cand_N = int(base.size)
        if 64 <= cand_N <= 10000:
            N = cand_N
        else:
            base = None
            N = 4498
    else:
        N = 4498

    S = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * N))
    fallback = make_fallback(N)

    if budget_s < 60.0:
        if base is not None and int(base.size) == N:
            return [float(x) for x in normalize_sum(base, S)]
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    candidates = []

    def add_candidate(arr):
        try:
            a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            if a.size != N:
                a = a.reshape(-1)[:N]
                if a.size != N:
                    return
            a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if a.sum() <= 0.0:
                return
            a = S * a / a.sum()
            if np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
                candidates.append(a)
        except Exception:
            return

    if base is not None and int(base.size) == N:
        add_candidate(base)
    add_candidate(np.full(N, S / float(N)))

    for a in (-4.0, -2.5, -1.0, 1.0, 2.5, 4.0):
        add_candidate(S * make_geometric_density(N, a))

    for i in range(10):
        try:
            dens = make_fourier_density(N, int(6 + 2 * i), rng, 0.25 + 0.17 * i)
            add_candidate(S * dens)
        except Exception:
            pass

    block_deadline = min(shutdown - 90.0, time.monotonic() + 700.0)
    if shutdown - time.monotonic() > 400.0:
        base_density = None
        if base is not None and int(base.size) == N:
            b = np.clip(base, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if b.sum() > 0.0:
                base_density = b / b.sum()
        try:
            block_seqs = run_block_phase(N, S, rng, block_deadline, base_density)
            for bseq in block_seqs:
                add_candidate(bseq)
        except Exception as ex:
            print("block phase failed:", ex, flush=True)

    scored = []
    for cand in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() + 2.0 >= shutdown:
            break
        try:
            s = float(cand.sum())
            if s <= 0.0:
                continue
            q = self_convolution(cand)
            sc = float(q.max())
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, cand.copy()))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if scored:
        scored.sort(key=_item_score)
        top = scored[:8]
    else:
        top = [(fast_score(fallback), fallback.copy())]

    print(f"Candidates ready: {len(scored)}; top score={top[0][0]:.9f}", flush=True)

    hall = []
    hall.append((top[0][0], top[0][1].copy()))
    for sc, cand in top[1:]:
        hall.append((sc, cand.copy()))

    # Give roughly equal time slices to the best starts, but leave room for
    # local search and exact verification.
    local_reserve = 180.0
    refine_deadline = min(shutdown - local_reserve, shutdown)
    if refine_deadline <= time.monotonic() + 20.0:
        refine_deadline = shutdown - 5.0

    idx = 0
    total = len(top)
    while idx < total and time.monotonic() + 30.0 < refine_deadline:
        _, start_raw = top[idx]
        remaining = max(20.0, refine_deadline - time.monotonic())
        per = remaining / float(total - idx)
        slot_deadline = min(refine_deadline, time.monotonic() + max(25.0, min(per, 900.0)))
        try:
            best_raw, best_sc = refine_full(start_raw, rng, slot_deadline, N, S)
        except Exception as ex:
            print("refine error:", ex, flush=True)
            continue
        if np.isfinite(best_sc):
            hall.append((float(best_sc), best_raw.copy()))
            if best_sc < hall[0][0]:
                hall.sort(key=_item_score)
            print(f"  after refine start {idx}: {best_sc:.9f} global_best={hall[0][0]:.9f}", flush=True)
        idx += 1

    # A quick local/rooting pass on the globally best sequence.
    local_deadline = min(shutdown - 25.0, time.monotonic() + local_reserve)
    if time.monotonic() + 20.0 < local_deadline:
        hall.sort(key=_item_score)
        best_sc, best_raw = hall[0]
        try:
            best_raw, best_sc = hill_climb_local(best_raw, rng, local_deadline, N, S)
        except Exception as ex:
            print("local error:", ex, flush=True)
        if np.isfinite(best_sc):
            hall.append((float(best_sc), best_raw.copy()))

    hall.sort(key=_item_score)

    # Final exact verification on a few best candidates.
    exact_deadline = shutdown - 3.0
    best_seq = [float(x) for x in hall[0][1]] if hall else [float(x) for x in fallback]
    best_exact = float("inf")

    tried = set()
    for sc, cand in hall[:10]:
        if time.monotonic() + 2.0 > exact_deadline:
            break
        # Cheap identity by rounded max/sum to avoid re-evaluating obvious copies.
        key = (float(cand[0]), float(cand[-1]), float(cand.sum()))
        if key in tried:
            continue
        tried.add(key)
        seq = [float(float(x)) for x in np.clip(np.asarray(cand), 0.0, 1000.0)]
        try:
            scor = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))  # noqa: F821
        except Exception:
            scor = float(sc)
        if np.isfinite(scor) and scor < best_exact:
            best_exact = scor
            best_seq = seq

    if not np.isfinite(best_exact) or best_exact >= 2.0:
        # Last-resort quick exact check on fallback/base.
        for seqarr in (fallback,):
            seq = [float(x) for x in seqarr]
            try:
                scor = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))  # noqa: F821
            except Exception:
                scor = float("inf")
            if np.isfinite(scor) and scor < best_exact:
                best_exact = scor
                best_seq = seq

    try:
        out = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        if out.size != N:
            out = out[:N] if out.size > N else np.concatenate([out, np.zeros(max(0, N - out.size))])
        out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
        out = np.minimum(out, 1000.0)
        if out.sum() < 0.01:
            out = make_fallback(N)
        out = np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64)
        return [float(x) for x in out]
    except Exception:
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]
```
